HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000129/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_13fe0adac761f995be86","text":"1. **A human rising that inverts the camel image.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ب ع ث/B001` is rousing a kneeling or restrained camel into motion; 91:13 introduces the she-camel and 91:14 cuts her down through `ع ق ر/B002`. **Structural cue and role:** the human subject rises first and then disables the animal that the root-image ordinarily rouses, making his motion a reversal of animation into immobilization. **Abductive move:** the camel branch and later animal are supplied; treating their sequence as deliberate inversion is inferred. **Reading change:** `انبعث` becomes a grotesquely misdirected activation, not neutral standing up.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000129/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5d2297b483eeef5be606","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:12:2\",\"root_000129\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Normalde çökmüş bir deveyi ayağa kaldırmayı çağrıştıran hareket, burada bir insanın kalkıp hemen ardından deveyi yere düşürmesiyle tersine çevrilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000563/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8f088d9dfeda013522a2","text":"2. **Dispatch without rightful sending.** Lexical evidence: ب عث B002 \"dispatching and directing\"; ر س ل B001/B002 sending/messenger in 91:13; ء ل ه B002 divine formula/name. Structural cue: a roused individual appears immediately before the messenger of Allah speaks. Assigned role: بعث creates a dark counter-sending, contrasted with the رسل mission. Abductive move: I infer two missions in collision: one unauthorized by collective rebellion, one authorized by Allah; the packet supplies the juxtaposition of انبعث and رسول الله. Reading change: the ayah reads as anti-messengerhood: a social impulse dispatches its agent against the divinely sent word.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000129/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_951363f7387d697d1cbb","text":"3. **Latent corruption becoming kinetic.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ب ع ث/B004` is setting out and pressing onward in good or evil; 91:10 `د س و/B003` is inward spoilage and 91:14 supplies the completed act. **Structural cue and role:** concealed self, collective denial, individual setting-out, warning, and violence form successive stages; `انبعث` is the conversion point from disposition to motion. **Abductive move:** the stages are supplied; the causal direction from inner spoilage to mobilized harm is inferred. **Reading change:** this brief ayah becomes the hinge at which a latent moral state acquires a body and trajectory.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000129/B002","root_000563/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9a00a696df8683fc9609","text":"2. **A counter-mission.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ب ع ث/B002` is dispatching a person, messenger, camel, or force toward a task; 91:13 `ر س ل/B002` names messenger and message. **Structural cue and role:** the self-launched `أشقاها` is immediately answered by a messenger bearing a protected claim, so dispatch supplies two opposed trajectories. **Abductive move:** the two movements are explicit, while calling the first a counter-mission to the second is relational inference. **Reading change:** the ayah marks not only an individual's initiative but an anti-dispatch set against the communicated order.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000129/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_aedc03d5067b2885034b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:12:2\",\"root_000129\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bu çıkış, hemen ardından gelen Allah'ın elçisinin haklı gönderilişine karşı yönelmiş yetkisiz bir karşı görev gibi işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c3fd532a2e972fdd8239","text":"The next two ayat made `انبعث` a counter-mission. Before reading 91:13, the roused wretched one could be only an impulsive criminal; after `رسول الله`, he becomes a socially dispatched anti-messenger whose movement collides with the authorized sending. The terrain branches in ط غ ي, ش ق و, and ع ق ب also made his wretchedness feel like movement along a hard slope toward consequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000808/B003","root_000937/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c8a8d51cf79f02bc0575","text":"4. **A perverse winner whose rise is temporary.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ش ق و/B003` is prevailing in a hardship contest and `ش ق و/B004` is a long rising mountain flank; 91:11 `ط غ ي/B005` supplies a high outcrop and 91:14 `س و ي/B001` equalizes. **Structural cue and role:** the superlative `أشقاها` rises from the group, occupies the action, and is followed by leveling, so competitive and topographic prominence converge in a short-lived role. **Abductive move:** the contest and terrain images are dormant and their convergence is exploratory; sequence supplies the rise and leveling but not a literal mountain. **Reading change:** \"most wretched\" can denote the one who wins a destructive competition and mistakes prominence for durable elevation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000808/B003","root_000808/B004","root_000937/B005","root_001033/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d7d52ec497f1b7d11c5b","text":"3. **Hardship contest and mountain flank.** Lexical evidence: ش ق و B003 \"prevailing in a hardship contest\"; ش ق و B004 \"long rising mountain flank\"; ط غ ي B005 high outcrop in 91:11; ع ق ب B012 steep pass in 91:15. Structural cue: the root after طغوى and before عقر/عقبها is one with both suffering and climbable terrain. Assigned role: أشقىها may be the one most sunk in hardship or the one who \"wins\" the contest of ruin. Abductive move: I infer that moral extremity is pictured as a contest/climb where the worst reaches the decisive position; the packet supplies contest and terrain branches. Reading change: \"most wretched\" becomes dynamic: the one who presses farthest along the hard slope of transgression.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000129/B001","root_000808/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d852cd5a89ffe373e31c","text":"1. **The wretched one as roused instrument of collective overflow.** Lexical evidence: ب ع ث B001 \"rousing a still thing into motion\"; ش ق و B002 \"hardship and strenuous suffering\"; ط غ ي B001/B002 overstepping/overflow in 91:11. Structural cue: `إذ` links the collective denial to the moment the most wretched is roused. Assigned role: انبعث is the activation event; أشقىها is the person in whom collective طغوى becomes motion. Abductive move: I infer that the group condition selects or energizes the individual agent; the packet supplies collective denial followed by singular rousing. Reading change: the ayah is not an isolated villain portrait but a mechanism of social transgression becoming embodied action.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000129/B001","root_000808/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e844981b147ba3da048a","text":"91:13 made `ب ع ث/B001` unexpectedly self-reversing: the human rises just before the she-camel appears, then 91:14 shows him participate in making her fall. The same later ayah levels the temporary elevation activated by `ش ق و/B004`, correcting prominence into a prelude to erasure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000808/B003","root_000808/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ede2c20b730031aa8740","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:12:3\",\"root_000808\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"En kötü kişi, zorluk yarışında öne çıkan ve yükselen bir dağ yamacına tırmanan kişi gibi, yıkıcı bir yarışın geçici galibi olarak görülebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000129/B001","root_000129/B004","root_000808/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f44288dcbf081156bf51","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:12:2\",\"root_000129\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]],[\"91:12:3\",\"root_000808\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"En kötü kişinin harekete geçmesi, topluluğun sınır aşan eğiliminin tek bir bedende yön ve hız kazanarak şiddete dönüşmesidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
